---

## 3. PRD flags (requirements I think are unrealistic or contradictory as written)

These are raised here and not quietly planned around. Each one needs a product or Expert decision.

- **F1. FR-SIM-7 / A5: sharing one scene across several plugin instances.** No DAW API exists for this. It would need IPC or shared memory between instances. That breaks under out-of-process hosting (Logic's AU hosting, Bitwig's sandboxing) and offline bounce, where instances render in arbitrary order and at arbitrary speed. *Recommendation:* for multi-stem, use **one instance with several stereo sidechain inputs**, where each input is a stem with its own emitters. This changes the PRD's A5. Deferred in any case.
- **F2. NFR-1: "match real impulse responses" for any scene.** This can only be measured for benchmark scenes where we have both geometry and a measured IR. For user scenes we can only claim "method evidenced on N rooms". Phase 05 makes this concrete, and the result may show that ISM plus a statistical tail falls short for some rooms. That's an input for the Expert, not something to tune away (NFR-8 forbids per-scene fudge factors). BRAS gives at most three usable rooms (145 / 2 350 / 8 650 m³) with estimated inputs, so Phase 05 results are evidence, not validation. A second measured dataset (measured RIRs with geometry and in-situ measured α, and s where possible; shoebox-like; including small rooms under 100 m³; licence allowing commercial use) is required before the Phase 13 beta decision: Maisara decides (PRD Q9), the researcher agent searches (RR-III is a lead, not a fact). It does not block Phase 05; if none is found, the beta decision says so (decider report 11, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05). Second source adopted: Arni + dEchorate (phase 05d; decider report 15). The measured-α target is not met by any open dataset (researcher reports 13–14). It stays the bar for the word 'validated'. Action: request the DELTA 2018 panel report from the Arni authors or FORCE/Flex Acoustics (Maisara). Commissioning a measured-α room: Maisara decides after 05/05d (input-matched differential within 1 jnd but large absolute AQ9 errors → input data dominate, Vorländer §11.5; differential > 1 jnd → method first, B12/B2) (decider report 15, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05).
- **F3. "Any environment" with one generic engine (PRD scene detail, NFR-8).** Chapels, mountainsides and cars aren't shoeboxes. They need mesh geometry and, for outdoor terrain, diffraction, which means a different simulation core (ray or beam tracing). v1 covers only shoeboxes, so v1 presets are shoebox approximations. "Outdoor field" works as a ground plane only (5 surfaces with α = 1.0). Mountains and real outdoor terrain come after v1.
- **F4. FR-LIC-2/3 and NFR-7: "no editing without a licence" vs. DAW automation.** Position parameters are exposed to the host (FR-3D-8). After a licence lapses, the user can still draw new automation in the DAW, and the plugin can't tell new automation from saved automation. Either automation keeps working, so "editing" is only blocked in the plugin UI, or parameters freeze, which breaks automated scenes the user saved. *Recommendation:* block UI edits only and accept automation. Decision needed before the licensing phase.
- **F5. FR-3D-4: "absorption, reflectivity".** These are the same physical quantity (reflectivity = 1 − α). The plan exposes absorption only (AQ3).
- **F6. "Atmosphere not modeled" vs. accuracy.** Air absorption matters from about 1 kHz in large spaces: in a 20×30×10 m room with T = 2 s it already shortens T by ≈5 % (≈1 EDT JND) at 1 kHz (Vorländer Eq. 4.32 with the Annex air table). The plan models it with a fixed standard atmosphere that users can't change (AX4). This keeps the PRD's intent of no user controls without losing accuracy.
- **F7. FR-SIM-4: ear photos for a personalized HRTF.** Photo-based HRTF estimation is still a research problem, and accuracy depends heavily on the method. A realistic first step is *importing a measured SOFA file*. Photo-based personalization should be treated as R&D or a third-party integration.
- **F8. FR-3D-9: "always facing the sources".** Under AX3 the head faces the emitters' horizontal centroid, not each emitter, so an individual emitter can still be beside or behind the listener (e.g. listener at the origin, L = (−5, 1), R = (5, 1) puts L at ≈79°). Orientation follows the centroid: a pair moving together around the listener keeps its frontal image, while an emitter circling alone does change its relative azimuth. This is a product consequence to confirm.
- **F9. Licensing deferred means v1 can't be sold.** The v1 in this plan is a technical slice or private beta. Commercial release needs the licensing phase (post-v1 backlog item B1).

---

## 4. Phases (v1)

Detailed, self-contained instructions live in `docs/phases/phase-XX.md`. Summary:

### Phase 00 — Scaffolding, CI, pluginval, empty plugin · M
- **Goal:** a pass-through stereo plugin that builds as VST3, AU and Standalone on all three OSes, passes pluginval in CI, and loads in a DAW.
- **Scope:** CMake + presets, pinned JUCE/Catch2 via FetchContent, library skeletons (`contract`, `sim`, `module3d`, `plugin`, `cli`), a Catch2 smoke test, GitHub Actions matrix (build, ctest, pluginval strictness 10, auval on macOS, ASan/UBSan on Linux), clang-format, build artifacts uploaded.
- **Out of scope:** any DSP, contract types, UI beyond the empty editor.
- **Dependencies:** none.
- **Deliverables:** building repo; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; plugin binaries as CI artifacts; build instructions in `CLAUDE.md` confirmed.
- **Acceptance (automated):** CI green on 3 OSes; pluginval exits 0 at strictness 10 on each; `auval -v aufx Spsc Mkrf` passes; a null test (output − input = 0 exactly) runs in ctest.
- **Acceptance (listening):** the plugin loads in Logic (AU), Ableton/Reaper (VST3, macOS) and a Windows DAW; audio passes through unchanged; saving and reopening the project works.
- **Risks:** AU registration on CI runners; JUCE/CMake version drift; Windows MSVC + Ninja environment setup.

### Phase 01 — Scene contract v1 · S
- **Goal:** the versioned 3D Module → Simulator contract, implemented and tested.
- **Scope:** `contract::SceneDescription` types per AQ1/AQ3/AX6/AX7; JSON round-trip; a validator that returns typed, human-readable errors; `contractVersion` parsing with major/minor rules; JSON Schema file `docs/contract/scene-v1.schema.json`; written spec `docs/contract/README.md`; example scenes in `tests/scenes/`; the boundary-check CI script.
- **Out of scope:** any rendering; mesh geometry; UI.
- **Dependencies:** 00.
- **Deliverables:** `src/contract/*`, schema, spec, ≥ 5 valid and ≥ 15 invalid fixtures.
- **Acceptance (automated):** round-trip is identical for all fixtures; each invalid fixture fails with its expected error code; the C++ validator and the JSON Schema agree on every fixture (checked by a Python test); an unknown major version is rejected and an unknown minor version is accepted with a warning; boundary check passes.
- **Acceptance (listening):** none. Instead, Maisara reads `docs/contract/README.md` and confirms it matches the product intent.
- **Risks:** over-designing for the future. Keep v1 minimal and rely on versioning.

### Phase 02 — Offline render CLI + acoustic analysis harness · M
- **Goal:** the measuring instruments, built *before* any DSP so every later phase can be checked automatically.
- **Scope:** `render_cli` (scene + input WAV → output WAV; `--ir` mode writes the IR), running a stub engine (dry pass-through) for now; Python package `tools/acoustics` with ISO 3382-1 metrics (Schroeder integration, T20/T30/EDT/C50/C80/D50/DRR per octave band), an IR comparison tool (arrival times, per-band level error), a click/discontinuity detector, a golden-file comparison with tolerances, a "listening pack" generator (renders test signals through test scenes into WAVs uploaded as CI artifacts), and test-signal generation (pink-noise bursts, clicks, sine sweeps, sustained low-frequency tones).
- **Out of scope:** real room simulation.
- **Dependencies:** 01.
- **Deliverables:** `src/cli/`, `tools/acoustics/`, pytest suite, CI job running it.
- **Acceptance (automated):** metric tools recover the known T60 of synthetic exponential-decay noise within ±2%; the click detector flags 100% of injected single-sample discontinuities ≥ −60 dBFS and gives no false positives on 60 s of clean, slowly modulated test tones; render_cli in stub mode produces a bit-exact copy of the input.
- **Acceptance (listening):** download the listening-pack artifact and confirm the test signals play and sound as described.
- **Risks:** metric implementation mistakes. Mitigate by validating against synthetic signals with known answers, and against `pyroomacoustics.experimental.measure_rt60` as a second opinion.

### Phase 03 — Early reflections: shoebox ISM · M
- **Goal:** a correct per-octave-band image-source IR for an omni receiver.
- **Scope:** ISM to `maxReflectionOrder`; per-band reflection coefficients √(1−α) with scattering removing the fraction s of specular energy, so the specular energy per reflection is (1−α)(1−s) (Vorländer Eq. 3.23); 1/r spreading; air absorption (AX4); band reconstruction into a broadband IR via a linear-phase or zero-phase-sum filterbank with perfect-reconstruction check; AX1 time alignment; plugged into render_cli.
- **Out of scope:** late tail; binaural; real-time.
- **Dependencies:** 01, 02.
- **Acceptance (automated):** arrival times match analytic image positions to ±1 sample; amplitudes match pyroomacoustics ISM (same geometry and absorption) within ±0.5 dB for the first 50 reflections; source↔receiver reciprocity holds (IR identical within −120 dB); symmetric rooms give symmetric IRs; α=1 on all walls gives the direct path only; filterbank sum of flat bands is a delta within −100 dB.
- **Acceptance (listening):** listening pack with clicks/speech-like bursts in 3 rooms (small, medium, large, all bands at α=0.3). The early echo pattern should get sparser and later as the room gets bigger, and at high order there should be no tonal "ringing" artifact apart from the expected flutter between parallel walls.
- **Risks:** the reconstruction filterbank smearing reflections; reflection explosion at high order (cap and benchmark).

### Phase 04 — Late tail + hybrid room IR · M
- **Goal:** a full-length room IR combining ISM early reflections with a statistical tail.
- **Scope:** per-band Eyring T60 with air absorption; seeded noise tail (deterministic from `tailSeed`) shaped per band; mixing time per emitter t_mix,e = the smallest t ≥ t_dir,e + max(20 ms, √V ms) at which the Hann window [t_dir,e + 2.5 ms, t] has expected energy-weighted k_eff ≥ 19; if the realised k_real < 19 there in any band 500 Hz–2 kHz, ISM-only up to an image-count cap measured against AQ10 (AX22 switch rule; zero-energy images pruned) (1 dB expected level precision; no corpus rule for the transition time, Vorländer §11.7; revised only on the Phase 05 handover evidence; the direct path is never faded or dropped); time-bounded image list (every image of any order before t_mix,e + 2.5 ms, `maxReflectionOrder` without effect on the hybrid IR, contract minor bump: AX20); tail level matched at t_mix,e from specular + AX16 scattered entries in the Hann-weighted window [t_dir,e + 2.5 ms, t_mix,e], each referred to t_mix,e along the decay and normalised to 10 ms, against the expected (not realised) unit-decay energy ē_b per 10 ms at t_mix,e (from emission), no accumulation from t = 0; expected σ_dB = 4.34/√k_eff, the realised non-zero entry count, k_real, realised σ and per-axis energy shares reported (not a bound in sparse or non-diffuse fields); a reported-only 10 ms envelope step across t_mix,e (AX21); equal-power ISM→tail crossfade (AX18); m_b = energy air-attenuation coefficient D/4.34; IR length = time until −90 dB in the slowest band (capped at 12 s; the cap is the only bound, see AX6, and longer decays are truncated).
- **Out of scope:** binaural tail decorrelation (Phase 08).
- **Dependencies:** 03.
- **Acceptance (automated):** T30 of the generated IR within ±5% of the Eyring prediction (250 Hz–4 kHz) across 6 test rooms, on AX11 ensemble-averaged decays (R per band in phase-04.md); a different seed gives the same T30 within ±2% (two independent AX11 ensembles); EDC is monotonic with no step larger than 1 dB at each emitter's ISM→tail transition; the same seed gives a bit-identical IR on the same platform; golden metrics stay within tolerance; 96 kHz and 44.1 kHz renders, compared on two per-rate AX11 ensembles, give the same T30 within ±2% at 63 Hz–8 kHz (R ≥ 4.5·σ₁², as the seed gate), the same EDT within ±2% at 63 Hz–8 kHz (R ≥ 9·(σ₁,44² + σ₁,96²)/tol², AX19; reported only until σ₁ is measured) and the same C80 within ±0.5 dB at 125 Hz–8 kHz (same R rule, AX19), single renders reported only.
- **Acceptance (listening):** decays sound smooth (no "grainy" or "metallic" tail); bright vs. dark material sets sound clearly different; no audible seam between early reflections and tail.
- **Risks:** an audible seam at the mixing time; Eyring being inaccurate for uneven absorption (known limitation, to note for the Expert); in sparse or non-diffuse fields the tail level has no stated accuracy beyond the realised-k switch and its cap (AX22; the switch is a jump point in source position where the position crossfade dips 3 dB at mid-fade, and up to ≈ 4.4 dB against the power-linear interpolation at a 10:1 power ratio; Phase 10 gates the maximum dip of the expected output power, computed from the two IRs at every ramp sample, at ≤ 1 dB (safe segment share f ≤ 0.41 at equal powers, 0.32 at 10:1, 0.21 for any ratio; decider report 11, FR-01); no diffuseness threshold: no code path or gate needs one, and the corpus gives none, Kuttruff §8.5; every tail's T60_b still uses Eyring, which presumes a diffuse field (Kuttruff §5.1), and the size of that error is measured by the Phase 05 decay-predictor comparison (B12), not by a threshold; former Expert question closed on 2026-11-05, decider, accepted by Maisara); no scattered energy before t_mix (known deficit, AX21; Maisara decided on 2026-11-05 that the early part should contain it, fix planned on Phase 05 evidence, §5 B11).

### Phase 05 — Comparison with measured IRs (evidence) · M
- **Goal:** the first real-world evidence for NFR-1 and AQ2, while changing the method is still cheap.
- **Scope:** script that downloads BRAS scenes by checksum; shoebox approximations of the most rectangular BRAS rooms, with geometry and materials taken from the dataset documentation (no hand tuning); comparison report `docs/validation/report-phase05.md` (generated) with T30/EDT/C80/D50 per band vs. measured; nightly CI job; a regression baseline. Reference comparisons in `tools/acoustics` (not `src/sim`, no method change): radiosity a* and γ²-Eyring decay predictors beside Eyring, a reference hybrid with early Lambert-scattered energy (AX21), the mixing-time handover against t_dir,e + 80 / 140 ms floors, and the humidity bound at 40 / 70 % RH with a validation-only atmosphere override (AX4).
- **Out of scope:** fixing shortfalls by per-scene tuning (forbidden by NFR-8).
- **Dependencies:** 04.
- **Acceptance (automated):** report generates reproducibly in CI; the nightly gate fails on any change of the simulated metrics against the committed baseline simulation, in either direction (T30/EDT by more than ±2 %, C80 by more than ±0.2 dB; sim–meas errors reported, not gated); each room's (mean over pairs) and each pair's pass/fail against AQ9 thresholds is printed, by α set; BRAS results are labelled evidence, not validation. Failing AQ9 doesn't fail CI. It fails the *phase review*, which escalates to the Expert.
- **Acceptance (listening):** an A/B of measured vs. simulated IR convolved with the same dry material, done with headphones (omni, so mono). Note where they differ, e.g. "simulated tail is brighter".
- **Risks:** BRAS documentation is too ambiguous to map to shoeboxes; real rooms don't fit the model. Both are useful findings.

### Phase 05e — Comparison with measured IRs, part 2 (remainder of 05) · M
- **Goal:** finish Phase 05 (split on 2026-11-06 at Maisara's request); every rule stays in `docs/phases/phase-05.md`, unchanged.
- **Scope:** with the plan decisions D-A (dimension rule, AX26), D-B (a* vs an independent particle Monte Carlo in all six cases, Kuttruff Table 5.2 and γ²-Eyring reported) and D-C (paired noise test, AX27, L_min = 51 dB), decided 2026-11-06 (§6; vetoable by Maisara): scenes for all selected rooms except CR4 (05f), i.e. CR2 and CR3, the CR2/CR3 documented-vs-model volume check (scope (a)), the baseline golden and green nightly gate, the committed report with EDC plots, the rest of phase-05 scope 6 (reference hybrid with B10 run, ΔL_b, handover, paired comparisons, t_mp50/t_mp95, B11 (B) evidence, B12), and the A/B pack in the listening-pack artifact, on CR2 + CR3 (provisional, two-room).
- **Out of scope:** as Phase 05; CR4 and everything that needs it (05f).
- **Dependencies:** 05.
- **Acceptance (automated):** the Phase 05 criteria not met in Phase 05, unchanged (listed in `docs/phases/phase-05e.md`).
- **Acceptance (listening):** as Phase 05.
- **Risks:** as Phase 05; AX26 changes the CR2 scenes; CR3's AX26 box plan exceeds its real footprint (raked seating is floor-assigned), so its early part / C80 are weak (reported, not tuned); the ±1 % a* gate may be borderline on Table 5.2 case 6 (solver at 1.25 m ≈ 1.2 % below a from-scratch particle simulation, still converging).

### Phase 05f — CR4 geometry and three-room re-run · M
- **Goal:** extract CR4 and complete Phase 05's evidence on three rooms (created 2026-11-06 at Maisara's request); every rule stays in `docs/phases/phase-05.md`, unchanged.
- **Scope:** decode the `CFaceTextureCoords` layout variant(s) in `skp_reader.py` (generic, file-format level, under the CR2/CR3 regression guard); extract CR4 and accept it only by the `bras_mapping.md` §2 acceptance test; on acceptance, CR4's reduction results, scenes, baseline extension, the three-room report replacing the provisional two-room one, the scope 6 room-set comparisons, and the deferred B12 and B11 decisions.
- **Out of scope:** anything that does not need CR4.
- **Dependencies:** 05e.
- **Acceptance (automated):** listed in `docs/phases/phase-05f.md` (reader regression guard, CR4 §2 acceptance test, `make_bras_scenes.py --check`, reproducible three-room report, nightly gate green with the CR4 baseline, AQ9 printed for CR4).
- **Acceptance (listening):** the A/B pack gains CR4 pairs, as Phase 05e.
- **Risks:** the SketchUp format is undocumented; the reader may need more than one layout. Stop rule after two sessions: `blocked (reader: CR4 not extracted)`, CR4 pending, not dropped; Maisara decides.

### Phase 05b — Early scattered energy (B11) · M
- **Goal:** promote the Phase 05 reference hybrid's early Lambert-scattered energy into `src/sim`, so the hybrid IR is no longer specular-only before t_mix (AX21), if and only if it passes the B11 promotion rule (decider report 8, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05).
- **Scope:** port of the mixed specular/diffuse propagation (§5 B11); independent Kuttruff §9.8 tracer in `tools/acoustics`; gates (A) and (B) of B11 with the window-level and tracer-bias branches; the AX16 tail-matching switch; `open_field` re-derivation; AQ10 W1/W2 measured and recorded. Session 2 ([phase-05b2](phases/phase-05b2.md)): the switch decided and specified (AX37–AX46); its implementation split to phase 05h (phase-05b2 §8).
- **Out of scope:** binaural directions of the scattered energy (08); per-band s (B10); tail decay source (B12).
- **Dependencies:** 04, 05, 05e, and 05f `done` or Maisara's recorded acceptance of the two-room label (the B11 promotion decision is deferred until CR4 is in, phase-05e scope (b)).
- **Acceptance (automated):** B11 (A) on all its cases; B11 (B) on the Phase 05 numbers; bit-identical offline renders; goldens updated as an intended change if promoted. Session 2's switch acceptance moved to phase 05h.
- **Outcome:** `done` (promoted) or `done (not promoted)` when (A) or (B) fails, with the reason recorded. Phase 08 builds on 05b in either outcome. Recorded: `done (not promoted; tail-level switch specified in phase-05h)`.
- **Risks:** (A) fails or the tracer is biased (B11 branches); too few uneven-α or low-s BRAS rooms (F2); rebuild time.

### Phase 05h — Tail-level switch implementation · L
- **Goal:** implement the B11 tail-level switch decided in phase 05b session 2: E_target from the propagated diffuse energy instead of the AX16 scattered entries (AX37), computed by the seeded Monte Carlo estimator (AX39–AX44); the early part stays specular-only (AX21). Phase file: [phase-05h](phases/phase-05h.md) (split from phase-05b2 §8, 2026-11-10).
- **Scope:** the `src/sim` estimator with the D1–D7 determinism package and the joint path-count rule; the AX38 diffuse-solve gate; the matching switch and diagnostics; the AX43 agreement suite, AX45 regression gate and AX46 bias check; the reported set, near-corner reference and M-2 tracer runs; goldens through `update_goldens.py`; BRAS re-run; W1/W2 with the diffuse share and the AX38 records; the C++ re-derivation of W_max and n\*_max; listening pack. Open decision D-H1 (margin between W_max and the blocking restore gate) before the gate is implemented.
- **Out of scope:** early rendering of scattered energy (not promoted); per-band s (B10); tail decay source (B12); a directional late tail (B18); Q2a (C), the async restore (phase 07c).
- **Dependencies:** 05b (`done`, with this split), 05f, 07.
- **Acceptance (automated):** agreement over 70 cases at z_FW 4.290 (AX42, AX43); the AX46 bias check; the AX45 gate; KAT/D5/cross-OS checks (AX39); U silent at every valid fixture default (AX44); the realised-work assertion (AX41); the 1 GB memory gate and the blocking 100 m, 96 kHz restore gate (AX38, AQ10); goldens via `update_goldens.py`; nightly BRAS green.
- **Risks:** D-H1 (no margin between W_max and the restore gate); n\*_max below 1 000 at the C++ cost (AX41); the outer-band tracer check failing in a band (AX37 (5.1)).

### Phase 05i — Tail-level switch: remainder of 05h · M
- **Goal:** finish what phase 05h could not carry in two sessions (CLAUDE.md split rule, 2026-11-10), under phase-05h's rules and atomicity. Phase file: [phase-05i](phases/phase-05i.md).
- **Scope:** D-H2 (c)'s remaining references in the committed priority order and the gating of whatever certifies (AX43, AX45, AX46); the AX38 (iv) records not produced in 05h and the K-e12 record (AX38 4c); the restore-gate growth-bound baselines and the register's medians from the first three-OS run, if 05h's CI run does not fit; the per-instance restore times in the (iv) loop if 07c condition 7 does not print them; the AX41 'pilot ships' list and the known U cases kept current.
- **Out of scope:** everything phase-05h §9 excludes; any change to AX37–AX46's rules.
- **Dependencies:** 05h.
- **Acceptance (automated):** each newly certified case-band gated through `update_goldens.py --extend` with its reason; the records printed in the CI job summary; nightly BRAS green.
- **Risks:** the 15 GB reference class not fitting the development machine (routes: the 16 GB-runner artifact job, or M-d, Maisara's action).

### Phase 05d — Second measured dataset (Arni, dEchorate) · M
- **Goal:** a second measured source beside BRAS (F2), with the one claim stronger than "evidence": the input-matched distribution test, "method evidence with the input bias cancelled exactly for Eyring (equal ᾱ at a common atmosphere, Vorländer Eq. 4.32); for distribution-sensitive models a common α error ε leaves a residual ε·(∂lnX_a/∂α − ∂lnX_b/∂α); not an NFR-1 validation" (decider report 15, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05; wording corrected by interrogator check 19, B5).
- **Scope:** `tools/validation/fetch_arni.py` and `fetch_dechorate.py` by checksum (Arni Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.6985104, omni LS01 campaign; dEchorate Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.6576203 v2.0, omni sources only, AQ8); mapping files `docs/validation/{arni,dechorate}_mapping.md` committed before any sim–meas number (α source rule, configuration subset, matched-pair list); Phase 05's generic reduction rules, metric conditions and report generator, unchanged except that the report also prints the EDT/C80 matched-pair d on Phase 05's reference-hybrid IR (tools only; decider report 16, Challenge 2, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05); AQ9 per configuration and per room by α set, the matched-pair differential, per-face reflection levels and FR-3D-3 coverage, reported; dEchorate arrival times scaled by c_meas/c_sim = 346.98/343, analysis-side; dEchorate < 300 Hz not evaluable.
- **Out of scope:** any simulator change; B11 (B) stays on BRAS (05b prints the 05d numbers as context).
- **Dependencies:** 05, 05e. 05b does not depend on 05d; 13 depends on 05d being `done` or `blocked (reason)`.
- **Acceptance (automated):** checksum check plus a tampered-file test; the report reproduces exactly; the nightly baseline includes the 05d scenes (same ±2 % T30/EDT, ±0.2 dB C80 change limits); a matched-pair selector unit test (every selected pair has equal per-material area totals and equal edge lengths, open–open and against perpendicular surfaces; at one common atmosphere, AX4 standard 20 °C/50 % RH, with ᾱ summed from the per-material area totals in a fixed material order, it gives an Eyring ΔT of exactly 0; a deliberately unmatched pair is rejected, the negative control; decider report 17, B1); the isolation-rule and mean |x| unit tests (phase-05d scopes 3 and 5). AQ9 misses do not fail CI.
- **Acceptance (listening):** none; Maisara reads the report summary and its labels.
- **Risks:** Arni's file layout makes even a subset impractical, or dEchorate has no omni source: Motus moves up, labelled "reduction-limited" (Motus and May et al. are reserves; SRIRACHA is a lead pending its licence); nightly runtime too long (run outside CI under phase-05's 24 h rule, K unchanged).

### Phase 06 — Real-time partitioned convolution + latency · M
- **Goal:** a real-time-safe convolution engine for multi-second IRs.
- **Scope:** uniformly partitioned overlap-save convolver (PFFFT, block = 256 or 512); N-input × M-output matrix (2×2 for v1); IR swap API with a two-slot crossfade (AQ4; equal-gain or equal-power per AX18); no allocation or locks on the audio thread; latency query; benchmark.
- **Out of scope:** plugin parameters (07); position smoothing policy (10).
- **Dependencies:** 02 (harness), 04 (IR generator for test IRs).
- **Acceptance (automated):** output matches direct (time-domain) convolution within −120 dB for random IRs/inputs at all supported block sizes, including host buffer sizes that aren't a multiple of the partition (1, 37, 64, 512, 2048); a crossfade between two IRs equals the analytic crossfade within −100 dB; allocation-tracking test shows zero heap allocations in `process()`; TSan clean; benchmark within the AQ10 CPU budget recorded in CI.
- **Acceptance (listening):** render_cli realtime-emulation of a drum loop through the medium room sounds identical to Phase 04's offline render (null test in CI already confirms this; just listen once).
- **Risks:** CPU cost for 12 s tails (consider non-uniform partitioning later; note it); denormals.

### Phase 07 — Plugin integration: parameters, state, scene → IR pipeline · M
- **Goal:** the plugin renders the room. Its positions and room settings are automatable parameters, and its state is saved in the project.
- **Scope:** `module3d::SceneModel` (owner of the editable scene) ↔ `contract::SceneDescription`; host parameters (listener x/y/z, L x/y/z, R x/y/z, room W/D/H, output trim, output mode placeholder) with stable IDs; scene → IR on a background builder thread (AQ4), synchronous in non-realtime mode; latency reported to the host; state = versioned plugin state wrapping scene JSON; state-migration test fixtures; generic JUCE editor for now (no custom UI).
- **Out of scope:** binaural (08); smoothing of fast automation beyond basic rebuild (10); custom UI (11).
- **Dependencies:** 01, 04, 06.
- **Acceptance (automated):** pluginval strictness 10 on 3 OSes, including its state and automation tests; save/restore gives a bit-identical render on the same platform (offline render of a fixed project before and after a state round trip); reported latency equals the measured onset offset to ±0 samples; an offline bounce rendered twice is bit-identical; parameter IDs are frozen by a test (renaming one fails CI); TSan clean.
- **Acceptance (listening):** in Logic and one VST3 host, moving the listener between near and far gives obviously more room and less direct sound when far; reopening the project restores the same sound; enable DAW delay compensation and confirm the track stays on the grid.
- **Risks:** host-specific state quirks (Logic AU vs. VST3); parameter ranges depending on room size (use normalized 0–1 within the room and document it).

### Phase 07c — Asynchronous diffuse solve · M
- **Goal:** move the diffuse solve off the synchronous state restore, prepare and `reset()` (Q2a (C), board answer J1, decider files 47 §4 (iii) and 51 §4 item 4), so W_max (AX41) is no longer sized to pluginval's restore watchdog. Phase file: [phase-07c](phases/phase-07c.md).
- **Scope:** conditions (iii) 1–8: timing; the 05h solve seam; W_max re-derived from the background budget (Seat 6's 3 s per emitter, the AX38 (iv) load mark, Table 15.1's 700 ms / 3 s rows), still a pinned work count; no real-time waiting (atomic swap with an AQ4 crossfade, one step per solve, never mid-drag, "settling", the ISM update never held behind the solve; cross-reference AX38 (v) and phase-10's update scheduling); the interim level (saved E_b, else the (A)-budget estimate or the previous level; never silence, never AX16 where it is > 1 dB off); offline waits and stays bit-identical; the restore gate re-scoped to restore without the solve; the Phase 12 (ii) re-run and Phase 08's capped-room golden tolerance.
- **Decisions first:** Seat 1's provisional-to-final step ruling; the interim-level and saved-E_b design (a state-version AX if E_b is stored); Seat 8's saved-state question for Seat 5; researcher questions (blocked `setStateInformation` tolerance, hosts without a reliable offline signal, host hang dialogs in Logic, Live, Cubase, Bitwig).
- **Dependencies:** 05h, 07. Before Phase 13's beta; ahead of Phase 08's golden freeze if Phase 08's heaviest mode fails the restore gate; forward on any restore-gate failure.
- **Acceptance (automated):** no synchronous diffuse solve on restore/prepare/`reset()`; the restore gate passes without the solve; atomic swap with crossfade, ISM never held; interim level never silence or a known-wrong AX16 level; offline bit-identical; W_max re-derived and pinned with one golden round.
- **Risks:** slipping past the first release without versioned estimator parameters (back to Maisara); the saved-state design (state version).

### Phase 08 — Binaural output with a generic HRTF · L
- **Goal:** headphone rendering: each reflection arrives from its true direction relative to the listener's head.
- **Scope:** SOFA → embedded binary converter (`tools/hrtf/`); licence check and attribution file; HRIR lookup with interpolation (nearest-neighbour baseline, then bilinear/spherical); listener orientation per AX3; per-image-source BRIR accumulation; binaural late tail with frequency-dependent diffuse-field interaural coherence; 2 emitters × 2 ears output; `outputMode=binaural`.
- **Out of scope:** personalized HRTF; head tracking (PRD out of scope).
- **From phase 05b (AX37, AX38, AX44):** E_b and t_mix,e only through `TailMatch`, bit-identical between omni and binaural and between the ears; a both-near binaural render; per-mode W1/W2 with the diffuse share; the AX38 (iv) records in binaural mode with the gate wait shown separately; item 8 with the gate wait included; may precede 05h on Seat 4's conditions (AX37 (5.3)).
- **Dependencies:** 04, 05b (`done` in either outcome; with "not promoted", 08 builds on the specular-only early part), 05h, 07. It may precede 05h on Seat 4's conditions (i)–(iv) per AX37 (5.3) (D-T5.3).
- **Acceptance (automated):** a source at +90° azimuth gives ITD 0.6–0.75 ms and a positive ILD above 2 kHz, mirrored at −90°; frontal median-plane sources give |ITD| < 20 µs; interaural coherence of the tail matches the diffuse-field model within ±0.1 per band; diffuse-field energy of the binaural IR matches the omni IR per band within ±1 dB; HRIR interpolation is continuous (no step > 1 dB in level as azimuth changes by 1°).
- **Acceptance (listening, headphones):** L/R emitters at ±30° in front sound in front and out of the head, not inside it; moving the listener close to the L emitter pulls the image left; the room sounds enveloping, not like stereo reverb; compare the generic HRTF's colouration and note it (expected).
- **Risks:** front/back confusion and in-head localization with a generic HRTF (known limitation, which motivates the deferred personalization); HRTF licence.

### Phase 09 — Stereo-speaker output mode · S
- **Goal:** `outputMode=stereoSpeakers` renders through the virtual ORTF pair (AQ7).
- **Scope:** per-image-source cardioid gains plus inter-capsule delay; tail decorrelated to the ORTF pair's diffuse coherence; mode switch is a host parameter (equal-power crossfade per AX18, uses Phase 06 swap); loudness matched to binaural mode.
- **Dependencies:** 07, 08 (shared accumulation path).
- **Acceptance (automated):** for a 5 m anechoic point source, the measured L−R level difference equals the value computed from exact geometry (each capsule's own angle to the source in the cardioid 0.5(1+cos ϑ) and its own distance in 1/r) within ±0.5 dB at φ ∈ {0°, ±30°, ±55°, ±90°}, positive for positive φ (toward the left capsule) and mirrored for negative φ; the left channel's level peaks within ±3° of the geometric prediction (≈+56.7° at 5 m); plane-wave sanity values 9.7 dB at 55° and 20.1 dB at 90° (±0.5 dB); a centre emitter gives equal L/R; integrated loudness (ITU-R BS.1770) of modes within ±1 dB for the reference scene; mono sum shows no comb-filter notch deeper than 6 dB below 2 kHz for a centre source.
- **Acceptance (listening, Adam monitors):** stable phantom centre; the room is audible on speakers; the mode switch during playback is smooth.
- **Risks:** users expecting the binaural sense of height or depth on speakers (set expectations in UI text).

### Phase 10 — Artifact-free movement under automation · M
- **Goal:** FR-SIM-6 / NFR-3. Automating positions never clicks, zips or glitches.
- **Scope:** parameter smoothing; update scheduling (cap of 1 rebuild per 20 ms, coalescing; effective interval max(cap, 30 ms crossfade, rebuild time) per AQ4); crossfade law per AX18 (equal-power for large jumps); tail cache when only positions change (the unmatched unit-energy decay; E_b, t_mix and the ISM→tail crossfade are recomputed every rebuild); crossfade policy; behaviour when rebuilding takes longer than the update interval (keep the last IR, never block); offline/realtime equivalence for static scenes; a **negative-control test**: a deliberately naive hard-switch build must be flagged by the click detector, which proves the detector can actually see the problem; the builder's wake-up on a parameter change (Phase 07 polls every 1 ms while idle) confirmed or replaced, with the worst-case extra delay of an isolated update stated.
- **Dependencies:** 06, 07, 08, 09.
- **Acceptance (automated):** across 20 automation scenarios (linear sweeps, jumps, oscillations at 0.1–5 Hz, full-room traversals), the click detector reports 0 events on sustained low-frequency test tones, in both output modes; the negative control is flagged; offline bounce of an automated scene is bit-identical across two runs; no IR update starves (≤ 100 ms worst-case staleness logged in the CI benchmark).
- **Acceptance (listening):** automate the source moving from 1 m to 15 m and back over 8 bars; also jump the listener between two corners every beat. Listen for clicks, zipper noise or level pumping. A slight smearing during fast moves is acceptable.
- **From phase 05b (AX38):** time waiting at the diffuse-solve gate counts in the staleness metric; stepwise room-size automation at large rooms documented; a kernel cache only through an AX that reopens AX38 (ii) and 4b.
- **Risks:** IR rebuild cost in large rooms (the AX20 image list grows with t_mix,e, not with `maxReflectionOrder`; reducing it while moving would change AX20 and need an ASSUMPTION entry if introduced).

### Phase 11 — Minimal placement UI · M
- **Goal:** a usable editor for room size, materials and positions. This is not the full 3D editor.
- **Scope:** top-down 2D room view plus a side elevation view; draggable listener, L and R emitters (drags write host automation gestures); room W/D/H fields; per-surface material dropdown (from Phase 12's library once present; placeholder list until then) plus an advanced per-band α/s table; output mode switch; latency and CPU display.
- **Out of scope:** 3D viewport, import, objects inside the room.
- **Dependencies:** 07, 09.
- **Acceptance (automated):** UI → scene binding tests run headless (simulated drag changes the right parameter by the expected amount); editor snapshot test renders without crash at 3 sizes; pluginval with editor open passes; the UI never writes to the scene off the message thread (asserted).
- **Acceptance (listening/use):** in each DAW, drag the source while playing (smooth); record a drag as automation and play it back (same movement); resize the editor; reopen the project.
- **From phase 05b (AX38, AX44):** the "approximate" note U (settled scene, info view, Seat 6 (i) text, the scene named as the cause for an unmoved emitter) and the U′ advanced diagnostic tier; "settling" on every instance whose latest change is not yet audible (queued at the gate, building or crossfading), project load included; no Eyring T60 shown as an open field's reverb time.
- **Risks:** scope creep toward the full editor; dependence on host UI scaling (Windows DPI).

### Phase 12 — Material library + starter presets · S
- **Goal:** FR-3D-3 within v1 limits. Editable starting scenes in the four categories, as data only.
- **Scope:** `resources/materials.json` (per-band α and s from published tables, with sources cited; scenes get one broadband s per surface, the mean of the 500 Hz–2 kHz table values, AX17); 2 presets per category, all shoebox (see F3): club, warehouse; small chapel, concert hall (shoebox); open field (ground only), courtyard; living room, car-cabin-sized room. Presets are scene JSON loaded through the same path as user scenes.
- **Dependencies:** 01, 11.
- **Acceptance (automated):** every preset validates and renders; a grep-based test confirms no preset name or ID appears in `src/sim/` (NFR-8); each preset's T30 falls in a plausible band documented per preset (e.g. living room 0.3–0.6 s).
- **Acceptance (listening):** go through all 8 presets with one track on headphones and speakers. Each should be recognisably different and plausible for its category, with open field sounding nearly dry with a ground reflection.
- **From phase 05b (AX37, AX38, AX44):** no preset's default placement raises U (if one does, the placement changes); each outdoor preset gets the two-branch check and the ghost-tail criterion, reported, not tuned; any D-T3 packet reaches Maisara before presets are built.
- **Risks:** material data licence/citation; shoebox chapels sounding unconvincing (expected, see F3).

### Phase 13 — v1 hardening: performance, DAW matrix, signing · M
- **Goal:** a v1 build fit for a private beta.
- **Scope:** profiling and optimization to AQ10; universal macOS binary (arm64 + x86_64); multi-instance stress (10 instances); sample-rate and buffer-size changes at runtime; denormal handling; state compatibility fixtures from every earlier state version; macOS code signing and notarization, Windows installer (signing optional); DAW compatibility matrix document; licences and attribution bundle (JUCE tier, PFFFT, HRTF, BRAS, Arni, dEchorate, materials); memory per instance measured and reported on all three OSes; the pinned pluginval release cached in CI.
- **Dependencies:** all previous.
- **Acceptance (automated):** full CI plus nightly validation green; benchmark meets AQ10 or the miss is recorded in the status table; notarization succeeds (`spctl -a -vv`); pluginval strictness 10 for the release build on 3 OSes.
- **Acceptance (listening/use):** fill in the DAW matrix (Logic, Ableton, Reaper on macOS; one or two DAWs on Windows): load, play, automate, bounce, reopen, change sample rate.
- **From phase 05b (AX38, AX41):** W_max and n\*_max re-checked on the release machines; phase-07c (the diffuse solve off the synchronous restore, Q2a (C)) is a beta prerequisite, otherwise the estimator parameters are versioned in saved state; multi-instance RSS on three OSes including Bitwig's sandbox.
- **Risks:** notarization and Apple developer account logistics; Windows Defender/SmartScreen without signing.

Replaces AX17's broadband reduction with 8 per-band s values per surface; Vorländer Annex scattering tables vary strongly with frequency. Contract **minor** bump, schema, README, fixtures. |
| B11 | Early scattered energy in the hybrid IR (fixes the AX21 deficit): at each specular hit, s(1−α) of the incident energy into the Lambert boundary patch hit, propagated with the mixed specular/diffuse form (at each later hit, s re-radiated per Lambert and 1−s continuing specularly: Joyce's reflection matrix, Kuttruff §4.6 Eq. 4.35, with the §5.2 split; Gilbert, §4.6, splits the boundary into specular and non-specular portions), the diffuse part gathered at the listener (Eq. 4.29), added before t_mix (Kuttruff Eq. 4.24) | NFR-1, FR-SIM-6 | **v1 phase 05b** (`docs/phases/phase-05b.md`; after 05, before 08) (decider report 8, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05) | **Promotion rule** (decider D4, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05): promote only if (A) and (B) both pass. **(A)** Independent reference, CI gate in the promotion phase: the rendered energy envelope (ISM specular + hybrid diffuse) against a seeded Kuttruff §9.8 partially diffuse ray tracer, ±1 dB per 10 ms bin up to t_mix,e, all 8 bands, gating only bins with k = (ΣE)²/ΣE² ≥ 684 (tracer 3σ ≈ 0.5 dB, systematic budget 0.5 dB; AX11 convention); at least 3 gated bins per case, and with fewer the case **fails** (it is not skipped). The tracer uses energy multiplication (Kuttruff §9.8 step 3a; Vorländer Eqs. 11.14–11.18), with a seed independent of the rendered side; its seed is not changed to pass. Engineering note, not from the corpus: each detection is time-stamped at the ray's closest approach to the receiver point, which removes the ±r_d/c entry-time smear (±1.5 ms) from the systematic budget. Particles: receiver radius r_d = 0.5 m, N₀ = ⌈254·V⌉ (Vorländer Eq. 11.12 with k = 684, Δt = 10 ms; ≈ 1.52·10⁶ at 6 000 m³); if a case has fewer than 3 bins with k ≥ 684, N is doubled, up to 16·N₀, after which the case fails; N, the seed and the doubling count are logged. Cases: s = 0.1, 0.25 and 0.4 with uniform α = 0.15 on all faces and all 8 bands in the 20×30×10 m room, AX4 air absorption on both sides; and Kuttruff Table 5.2 layouts 4, 5 and 6 (10×20×30 m, in Table 5.2's notation) at s = 0.1, 0.25 and 0.4 plus the s = 1 anchor, with the solver called directly (decider report 7, FE-02, DN1, DN2; DN3, DN4 verified by the interrogator, report 6). Also compare the matching level directly with the tracer, per case and per band 500 Hz–2 kHz: ΔL_b,tr = 10·log10(E_target(AX16)/E_target(tracer)), and the hybrid's E_target against the tracer's, within ±1 dB, in the same Hann window, with tracer k ≥ 684 over the window. (i) If the hybrid's window level passes, tail-level matching switches to the hybrid whenever |ΔL_b,tr| > 1 dB anywhere, even if the per-bin render gate fails and early rendering is not promoted. (ii) If it fails, nothing switches: AX16 records ΔL_b,tr per case as a known deficit, and the diagnosis goes to Maisara. (iii) If Phase 05 accepted X-b (≤ 1 dB) but |ΔL_b,tr| > 1 dB anywhere, the acceptance is reversed and (i)/(ii) apply. The switch is valid only with SE(ΔL_b,tr) = 4.34/√k_tr ≤ 1/3 dB (threshold/3, AX11); k ≥ 684 gives ≤ 0.17 dB; realised k and SE are printed; the tracer seed is fixed, so the decision reproduces on re-run (decider report 11, FR-04 and C1, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05). Tracer bias: diagnose with two tracer self-checks against independent anchors: s = 0 against the ISM image energies in 10 ms bins (the lattice is exact, Kuttruff §4.1), and s = 1 against the Table 5.2 iteration T. Fix whichever side fails. Then halve the hybrid's time step and patches until the change is < 1 % (as in D2). If the remaining bias is ≤ 0.5 dB, it is within the stated systematic budget: the gate is unchanged and the bias is recorded. If it is > 0.5 dB in any gated bin, (A) fails and the branch above applies. N (beyond the pre-stated doubling rule), seeds and bins are never changed after results are seen (decider report 8, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05). Also re-derive the Phase 04 `open_field` check against the Lambert estimate (−23 to −43 dB per band, ledger OF). **(B)** BRAS paired check on the Phase 05 numbers: **not worse**, i.e. the room-averaged AQ9 error (EDT and C80, 500 Hz–2 kHz, equal room weight) with the hybrid is not higher by more than δ = 0.25 jnd for either metric (Δ̂ ≤ +δ), and no room's Δ̂ exceeds +1 jnd on either, each with SE ≤ threshold/3 from seed-paired ensembles sized per phase-05 scope 6 "Paired comparisons" (pilot n ≥ 51, then R ≥ max(144·σ₁,Δ², 9·σ₁,Δ,room², 9·σ₁,Δ,pair²), σ in jnd; decider report 7, FE-01, DN5). Vorländer Table 6.1 states these jnds for 500–1000 Hz only; at 2 kHz they are applied by extension (AQ9). The 500–1000 Hz mean is printed as the in-range figure. The per-room gain is reported sorted by absorption contrast, largest single-face share and broadband s, with no threshold. (B) is a safety check on estimated input data (researcher findings F2, F6, F7, BRAS documentation, outside the corpus), not a validation; n_rooms ≤ 3, so m ≤ 8 comparisons, false-failure ≈ 1.1 % (decider report 11, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05). With too few uneven-α or low-s BRAS rooms no minimum count applies; the limitation is recorded in the Phase 05 report and `bras_mapping.md` (F2). With no evaluable EDT/C80 pair, promotion needs Maisara's explicit sign-off on (A) alone. Rebuild time is measured against AQ10 and recorded; it does not block promotion. A check at s = 1 alone is circular. If the Phase 05 |ΔL_b| exceeds 1 dB anywhere, this phase also switches tail-level matching to the hybrid's propagated diffuse energy (AX16). Changes AX16, AX21; no contract change. Maisara decided on 2026-11-05 that the early part should contain scattered energy. **Phase 05b outcome:** early rendering not promoted (gate (B) failed; gate (A) not evaluated, compute limit, AX36), so AX21 stands; the tail-level switch is decided and specified in phase-05b2 (AX37–AX46; R1 certified at 2.5 m in CR2, CR3 and CR4 at 500 Hz–2 kHz at the AX28 / Phase 05 positions, R2 = D-T1.1 = AX39, AX36 (c); accepted by Maisara 2026-11-10). |
| B12 | Tail decay source other than Eyring (radiosity a* or another predictor) | NFR-1, Q2 | new phase after 05 | Decision rule (decider report 8, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05): Phase 05 compares predicted T₆₀ (deterministic, so no seed noise, but not error-free: a* is converged to < 1 % per patch halving and the γ² choice moves γ²-Eyring by ≈ 0.4–3.6 % (Kuttruff Eqs. 5.33–5.34), both the size of the 1.25-point margin) with measured T30 at 500 Hz–2 kHz, room-averaged. A predictor replaces Eyring for all scenes (NFR-8: one predictor, no per-room choice) only if it lowers the mean \|error\| by ≥ 0.25 working jnd (1.25 % points) and makes no room worse by > 1 jnd (5 %). Otherwise Eyring stays. Shown separately for uniform and uneven α. Conditions (decider report 11, accepted by Maisara, 2026-11-05): (i) a predictor counts as lowering the error only if it does so with a* converged to < 0.4 % per halving (margin/3, by analogy with AX11); (ii) a predictor with a free modelling input (γ²) must meet the rule at every admissible value: the Table 5.1 row and 0.4; (iii) the 5 % T jnd is a working value from outside the corpus (Vorländer Table 6.1 gives 5 % for EDT, not for T); (iv) only the initial-estimate α set counts: the fitted set is circular in Eyring's favour by construction (its α were scaled so that Eyring matches the measured T; researcher finding F6, BRAS documentation, outside the corpus) and is reported only; (v) a* may not replace Eyring while ledger DB-1 (Table 5.2 / Fig. 5.10 case-4 gap) is unresolved; the AQ2 change request states this (D-B, decider 2026-11-06); e.g. Table 5.2 case 4 (absorbing floor): Eyring 1.38 s is 14 % above the Iteration value 1.21 s; under (v) Eyring stays for such rooms. An outcome from BRAS alone is labelled "input-limited" in the AQ2 change request, and Maisara decides on it (C2). Implemented through an AQ2 change. Not a* by default: a* assumes Lambert (s = 1) reflection on all walls (Kuttruff §5.7 Eqs. 5.49–5.56; §2.7, §5.2 Eq. 4.24). Phase 05f outcome (CR2, CR3, CR4; initial α; both meters; recommendation under C2, decider 2026-11-07; **accepted by Maisara 2026-11-07**): a* fails (i) and (v). γ²-Eyring meets the rule at the only admissible γ² (0.4; no BRAS box is tabulated in Kuttruff Table 5.1): −1.83 / −1.82 pts, worst room −1.22 pts; the gain grows with γ² (0.342: −1.56; 0.613: −2.87). **Recommended: not adopted; Eyring stays; AQ2 unchanged**, because the BRAS margin does not separate accurate from inaccurate predictors: Eyring under-predicts T in 8 of 9 room-bands, so any lengthening lowers the mean error, and Sabine, less accurate than Eyring on Kuttruff Table 5.2 (mean \|err\| ≈ 14.3 % vs ≈ 10.3 %), gains most (≈ 4.5 pts). Eq. 5.34 lengthens T for every γ² > 0 (Kuttruff §5.5) and fails this rule on Table 5.2 (lowers the mean by ≈ 0.1 pts; case 4 at γ² = 0.4: 1.47 s vs Eyring 1.38 s and Iteration 1.21 s, worse by ≈ 7.8 pts); γ² ≈ 0.4 is stated for rooms not far from a cube, and Eq. 5.30's condition σ_N²a²/2 ≪ 1 is not met over most of the T30 range (≈ 0.08 at −5 dB rising to ≈ 0.55 at −35 dB in case 4). Known cost of keeping Eyring: it is −22 % on Table 5.2 case 6, and absorption concentrated on the floor (audience) tends to put T below Eyring in auditoria (Kuttruff §5.7); γ²-Eyring makes such rooms worse and leaves case 6 at ≈ −21 %. **User-audible fact for Phase 13's beta list:** on initial α, the simulated T30 at 500 Hz–2 kHz misses the measured value by a room-mean \|error\| of 16.1 / 21.0 / 22.1 % (≈ 3.2–4.4 working jnd; condition iii); it is short in every band in CR2 and CR4 and mixed in CR3 (+22 % at 500 Hz, −12 / −29 % at 1–2 kHz). No predictor tested brings the predicted T within 10 % in any room (room means: Sabine 13.4–16.6 %, a* 14.9–17.7 %, γ²-Eyring 15.2–19.7 %, Eyring 16.4–22.8 %); the predictor moves the error by at most ≈ 8 pts, so most of the residual comes from input plus method. Reopen only on a measured dataset where (1) every face carrying ≥ 10 % of A in any AQ9 band has α measured independently of that room's T (product threshold), (2) Sabine does not also pass, and (3) γ²-Eyring passes at every admissible γ² including a room whose largest face carries the largest share of A. Phase 05d (Arni, dEchorate) is evidence, not validation, and counts only for rooms that its α-source rule shows meeting (1). (Ledger F5f-2 … F5f-26.) |
